import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import manifest from "../../../../ref/design/brand/generated/manifest.json";
import { referencePath } from "../reference-path";

type Background = "light" | "dark" | "transparent";
type ImageState = "present" | "delayed" | "blocked";

const assets = [
  { id: "Creator Signal horizontal", base: "creator-signal-logo-horizontal" },
  { id: "Creator Signal stacked", base: "creator-signal-logo-stacked" },
  { id: "Sales Pulse endorsement", base: "sales-pulse-by-creator-signal-horizontal" },
  { id: "Sales Pulse mark", base: "sales-pulse-mark" }
] as const;

function variantFor(background: Background) {
  if (background === "dark") return "reversed";
  if (background === "transparent") return "one-colour";
  return "light-background";
}

function BrandAssetGallery({ background, imageState }: { background: Background; imageState: ImageState }) {
  const variant = variantFor(background);
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Brand assets · governed output #{manifest.issue}</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Approved identities, rendered from the generated pipeline</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">
        This gallery loads generated delivery assets, not copied Storybook artwork. The manifest currently governs {manifest.outputs.length} technical outputs. Admin and Content use the Creator Signal lockup with a live-text product label.
      </p>
      <section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid" aria-label={`${background} background brand examples`}>
        {assets.map((asset) => (
          <article className="cs-reference-card" key={asset.base}>
            <div className="cs-reference-brand-stage" data-background={background} data-image-state={imageState}>
              <img
                alt={asset.id}
                src={referencePath(`/brand-assets/delivery/${asset.base}/${variant}.svg`)}
              />
              <span className="cs-reference-brand-fallback">{asset.id}</span>
            </div>
            <strong>{asset.id}</strong>
            <code>{asset.base}/{variant}.svg</code>
          </article>
        ))}
        <article className="cs-reference-card">
          <div className="cs-reference-brand-stage" data-background={background} data-image-state={imageState}>
            <img alt="Creator Signal" src={referencePath(`/brand-assets/delivery/creator-signal-logo-horizontal/${variant}.svg`)} />
            <span className="cs-reference-brand-fallback">Creator Signal</span>
          </div>
          <strong>Creator Signal <span aria-label="Product label">Admin</span></strong>
          <span>Generic brand lockup plus live product label</span>
        </article>
        <article className="cs-reference-card">
          <div className="cs-reference-brand-stage" data-background={background} data-image-state={imageState}>
            <img alt="Creator Signal" src={referencePath(`/brand-assets/delivery/creator-signal-logo-horizontal/${variant}.svg`)} />
            <span className="cs-reference-brand-fallback">Creator Signal</span>
          </div>
          <strong>Creator Signal <span aria-label="Product label">Content</span></strong>
          <span>Generic brand lockup plus live product label</span>
        </article>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { Badge, KeyValueItem, KeyValueList, Notice, Panel } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import { EMAIL_TEMPLATE_DEFINITIONS, renderEmailTemplate, type EmailTemplateCode } from "@creator-signal/email";
import emailInventory from "../../../../design-system/email-surfaces.json";

type EmailSurface = {
  id: string;
  system: string;
  provider: string;
  ownership: string;
  renderer: string;
  dataClassification: string;
  providerLimitations: string[];
  templates: string[];
  storybook: { coverage: "contract" | "rendered"; storyId: string };
};

const inventory = emailInventory as { surfaces: EmailSurface[] };

const fixtureVariables: Record<EmailTemplateCode, Record<string, string>> = {
  "workspace.export_ready": {
    request_reference: "fixture-export-001",
    expires_at: "31 August 2026 at 5:00 pm AEST",
    download_url: "https://example.test/export/fixture-export-001"
  },
  "workspace.deletion_requested": {
    request_reference: "fixture-delete-001",
    received_at: "24 August 2026 at 9:30 am AEST"
  },
  "billing.payment_failed": {
    workspace_reference: "fixture-workspace-001",
    portal_url: "https://billing.stripe.com/p/session/test_fixture"
  }
};

function GalleryShell({ children, title }: { children: React.ReactNode; title: string }) {
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Canonical email gallery · synthetic only · no delivery</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">{title}</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">The inventory and owned renderers are authoritative. This Storybook gallery is their inspectable, non-delivering projection; provider-controlled output is described as a contract rather than copied markup.</p>{children}</main>;
}

function SurfaceContract({ surface }: { surface: EmailSurface }) {
  return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><Badge tone={surface.storybook.coverage === "rendered" ? "success" : "info"}>{surface.storybook.coverage === "rendered" ? "Rendered from owned source" : "Provider contract"}</Badge><h2>{surface.id}</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Trigger owner" value={surface.ownership} /><KeyValueItem label="Renderer or provider" value={surface.renderer} /><KeyValueItem label="Data classification" value={surface.dataClassification} /><KeyValueItem label="Delivery" value="Synthetic Storybook fixture only; no message is sent." /><KeyValueItem label="Templates" value={surface.templates.join(", ")} /></KeyValueList><h3>Limitations</h3><ul>{surface.providerLimitations.map((limitation) => <li key={limitation}>{limitation}</li>)}</ul></Panel>;
}

function TransactionalRenderedExamplesView() {
  const templates = Object.keys(EMAIL_TEMPLATE_DEFINITIONS) as EmailTemplateCode[];
  return <GalleryShell title="Transactional rendered examples"><section className="cs-story-section"><Notice heading="Synthetic, non-delivering fixture" tone="info"><p>These HTML and plain-text examples call the real <code>@creator-signal/email</code> renderer using only <code>example.test</code> or synthetic values.</p></Notice></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid">{templates.map((code) => {
    const rendered = renderEmailTemplate(code, fixtureVariables[code]);
    return <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={code}><h2>{code}</h2><p><strong>Subject:</strong> {rendered.subject}</p><iframe sandbox="" srcDoc={rendered.html} style={{ border: "1px solid currentColor", minHeight: "32rem", width: "100%" }} title={`Synthetic ${code} email HTML`} /><h3>Plain text</h3><pre>{rendered.text}</pre></Panel>;
  })}</section></GalleryShell>;
}

function ProviderContracts({ ids, title }: { ids: string[]; title: string }) {
  return <GalleryShell title={title}><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid">{inventory.surfaces.filter((surface) => ids.includes(surface.id)).map((surface) => <SurfaceContract key={surface.id} surface={surface} />)}</section></GalleryShell>;
}

  tags: ["autodocs"],

./src/stories/DashboardExplainability.stories.tsx:
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { Badge, EmptyState, ErrorState, KeyValueItem, KeyValueList, LoadingIndicator, Notice, Panel, Skeleton, Stat, StatGroup, StatusIndicator } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import { adminRegisteredCustomersMetric, proCategoryLeadRule, proNetEarningsMetric } from "@sales-pulse/analytics";
import { dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue } from "@sales-pulse/dashboard-contracts";
import catalogue from "../../../../dashboard-explainability/catalogue.json";

type DataPoint = { id: string; label: string; sourceRefs: string[]; metricRefs: string[]; ruleRefs: string[] };
type Widget = { id: string; label: string; classification: string; presentationOnly?: boolean; component: string; selector: string; api?: { endpoint: string; responseFields: string[]; responseTypes: string[] }; filters: string[]; supportedStates: string[]; dataPoints: DataPoint[] };
type Section = { id: string; label: string; selector: string; widgets: Widget[] };
type Dashboard = { id: string; label: string; classification: string; lineageConfidence: string; route: string; component: string; selector: string; sections: Section[] };
type Definition = { name: string; explanation: string; semantics: { window: string; timezone: string; freshness: string; missingData: string }; authority?: string };
type ReferenceState = "normal" | "loading" | "empty" | "warning" | "error" | "entitlement" | "stale";

const generatedCatalogue = catalogue as unknown as { dashboards: Dashboard[] };
const definitions = new Map<string, Definition>([
  ["revenue.net@1", proNetEarningsMetric],
  ["signal.category-lead@1", proCategoryLeadRule],
  ["admin.registered_users@1", adminRegisteredCustomersMetric]
]);

const sourceById = new Map(dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue.sources.map((source) => [source.id, source]));
const acquisitionBySource = new Map(dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue.acquisitionPaths.map((path) => [path.sourceRef, path]));

function pilotWidgets() {
  return generatedCatalogue.dashboards.flatMap((dashboard) => dashboard.sections.flatMap((section) => section.widgets.map((widget) => ({ dashboard, section, widget })))).filter(({ widget }) => Boolean(widget.api));
}

function stateContent(state: ReferenceState) {
  if (state === "loading") return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><LoadingIndicator label="Loading synthetic dashboard data" /><Skeleton /><Skeleton /></Panel>;
  if (state === "empty") return <EmptyState title="No synthetic records in this period" description="The reference shows the empty state without inventing a value." />;
  if (state === "warning") return <Notice heading="Partial synthetic evidence" tone="warning"><p>One source is delayed. Labels and timestamps make the limitation explicit; colour is not the only signal.</p></Notice>;
  if (state === "error") return <ErrorState title="Synthetic source unavailable" description="No data was replaced or estimated. Try again when the source is available." />;
  if (state === "entitlement") return <Notice heading="Synthetic entitlement boundary" tone="warning"><p>This widget is unavailable until the required plan or operator permission is present.</p></Notice>;
  if (state === "stale") return <Notice heading="Synthetic stale result" tone="info"><p>This view uses an older generated timestamp and must be labelled before use.</p></Notice>;
  return <Notice heading="Synthetic populated state" tone="success"><p>Example values are invented review fixtures, never customer data.</p></Notice>;
}

function Lineage({ dashboard, widget }: { dashboard: Dashboard; widget: Widget }) {
  const point = widget.dataPoints[0]!;
  const sourceRef = point.sourceRefs[0] ?? "";
  const source = sourceById.get(sourceRef);
  const acquisition = acquisitionBySource.get(sourceRef);
  const calculation = [...point.metricRefs, ...point.ruleRefs].join(" + ") || "configuration-only presentation";
  return <div className="cs-lineage" role="img" aria-label={`${dashboard.label}, ${widget.label}: dashboard to widget to API to calculation to storage to acquisition lineage`}><strong>Generated lineage (text alternative included)</strong><ol><li>{dashboard.label}</li><li>{widget.label}</li><li>{widget.api?.endpoint ?? "No API; static configuration"}</li><li>{calculation}</li><li>{source?.location ?? point.sourceRefs.join(", ")}</li><li>{acquisition?.label ?? "Repository configuration"}</li></ol></div>;
}

function DashboardSummary({ dashboard }: { dashboard: Dashboard }) {
  const widgets = dashboard.sections.flatMap((section) => section.widgets);
  return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Generated dashboard summary</p><h2>{dashboard.label}</h2><p>{dashboard.classification === "repository_configured_upstream" ? "Presentation-only configured operator links. No unavailable upstream internals are inferred." : "Application-owned surface described by accepted manifest fields and identifiers."}</p><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Stable ID" value={<code>{dashboard.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Route" value={<code>{dashboard.route}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Source component" value={<code>{dashboard.component}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Manifested widgets" value={String(widgets.length)} /></KeyValueList></Panel>;
}

function WidgetReference({ dashboard, section, widget, state = "normal" }: { dashboard: Dashboard; section: Section; widget: Widget; state?: ReferenceState }) {
  const point = widget.dataPoints[0]!;
  const sources = point.sourceRefs.map((id) => sourceById.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const acquisitions = point.sourceRefs.map((id) => acquisitionBySource.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const definitionEntries = [...point.metricRefs, ...point.ruleRefs].map((reference) => [reference, definitions.get(reference)] as const);
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Dashboard explainability · synthetic static reference</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">{widget.label}</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">{dashboard.label} / {section.label}. This page is generated from accepted dashboard manifests, metric/rule contracts and source/acquisition contracts. It never connects to a customer session or provider.</p><section className="cs-story-section"><Lineage dashboard={dashboard} widget={widget} /></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid"><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Purpose and visible part</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Widget ID" value={<code>{widget.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Data point ID" value={<code>{point.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Rendered selector" value={<code>{widget.selector}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="API fields" value={<ul className="cs-reference-code-list">{widget.api?.responseFields.map((field: string) => <li key={field}><code>{field}</code></li>)}</ul>} /></KeyValueList></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Window, freshness and caveats</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Filters" value={widget.filters.length ? widget.filters.join(", ") : "No user-selected filters"} /><KeyValueItem label="Supported states" value={widget.supportedStates.join(", ")} /><KeyValueItem label="Confidence" value={dashboard.lineageConfidence} /><KeyValueItem label="Caveat" value={widget.presentationOnly ? "Presentation-only configuration; no product metric is claimed." : "Synthetic examples preserve the contract and do not show customer records."} /></KeyValueList></Panel></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Calculation and version</h2><div className="cs-reference-grid">{definitionEntries.map(([reference, definition]) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={reference}><Badge tone={definition ? "success" : "neutral"}>{reference}</Badge><h3>{definition?.name ?? "Supporting contract"}</h3><p>{definition?.explanation ?? "This supporting reference remains explicitly named but is outside the versioned pilot definition set."}</p>{definition && <KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Authority" value={definition.authority ?? "rule"} /><KeyValueItem label="Window" value={definition.semantics.window} /><KeyValueItem label="Timezone" value={definition.semantics.timezone} /><KeyValueItem label="Freshness" value={definition.semantics.freshness} /><KeyValueItem label="Missing data" value={definition.semantics.missingData} /></KeyValueList>}</Panel>)}</div></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Source and acquisition evidence</h2><div className="cs-reference-grid">{sources.map((source) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={source!.id}><h3>{source!.id}</h3><p><code>{source!.location}</code></p><p>{source!.confidence} · {source!.sensitivity}</p></Panel>)}{acquisitions.map((path) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={path!.id}><h3>{path!.label}</h3><ol>{path!.steps.map((step) => <li key={step}>{step}</li>)}</ol><p><strong>Evidence:</strong> <code>{path!.evidenceLocation}</code></p></Panel>)}</div></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Synthetic isolated-widget state</h2>{stateContent(state)}</section></main>;
}

function DashboardOverview() {
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Dashboard explainability · synthetic static reference</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">Dashboard parts and lineage</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">This reference is a generated review aid. It intentionally separates internal source paths from later customer-safe product copy.</p><section className="cs-story-section"><StatGroup><Stat label="Manifested dashboards" value={String(generatedCatalogue.dashboards.length)} context="Generated from application-owned manifests" /><Stat label="Pilot widgets" value={String(pilotWidgets().length)} context="Each has a focused reference" /></StatGroup></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid">{generatedCatalogue.dashboards.map((dashboard) => <DashboardSummary dashboard={dashboard} key={dashboard.id} />)}</section><section className="cs-story-section"><Notice heading="Synthetic fixtures only" tone="info"><p>All example states use invented labels and values. No credentials, private endpoint values, customer data or production screenshots are included.</p></Notice></section></main>;
}

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { AppI18nProvider } from "../../../web/i18n/client";
import {
  ShopOverviewDashboardView,
  type OverviewDashboardData,
  type OverviewDashboardVariant
} from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/overview/shop-overview-client";
import { SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE } from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/overview/shop-overview.fixture";

type PreviewState = "loading" | "updating" | "empty" | "error" | "ready";

function fixtureFor({ imageFallback, longLabel }: { imageFallback: boolean; longLabel: boolean }): OverviewDashboardData {
  const first = SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items[0]!;
  return {
    ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE,
    bestSellers: {
      ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers,
      items: [{
        ...first,
        designName: longLabel
          ? "A deliberately long synthetic Spoonflower design name for narrow layouts and two hundred percent zoom"
          : first.designName,
        thumbnailUrl: imageFallback ? null : first.thumbnailUrl
      }, ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items.slice(1)]
    }
  };
}

function DashboardPreview({ imageFallback = false, longLabel = false, state = "ready", variant = "overview" }: {
  imageFallback?: boolean;
  longLabel?: boolean;
  state?: PreviewState;
  variant?: OverviewDashboardVariant;
}) {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"30" | "month" | "ytd" | "lastyear" | "all">("ytd");
  const [chartMode, setChartMode] = useState<"revenue" | "orders" | "both">("both");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<"Wallpaper" | "Fabric" | "Home decor" | "">("");
  const [format, setFormat] = useState("");
  const [highlightIndex, setHighlightIndex] = useState(0);
  const [accent, setAccent] = useState<"pink" | "orange" | "periwinkle" | "green" | "sand">("pink");
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<"thumbnails" | "rows" | "columns">("thumbnails");
  const hasData = state !== "loading" && state !== "error";
  const data = hasData ? fixtureFor({ imageFallback, longLabel }) : null;

  return (
    <AppI18nProvider locale="en-AU">
      <ShopOverviewDashboardView
        accent={accent}
        category={category}
        chartMode={chartMode}
        data={data}
        {...(state === "error" ? { errorMessage: "Synthetic Storybook error. No marketplace request was made." } : {})}
        format={format}
        highlightIndex={highlightIndex}
        layout={layout}
        onAccentChange={setAccent}
        onCategoryChange={(value) => { setCategory(value); setFormat(""); }}
        onChartModeChange={setChartMode}
        onFormatChange={(value, nextFormat) => { setCategory(value); setFormat(nextFormat); }}
        onHighlightChange={setHighlightIndex}
        onLayoutChange={setLayout}
        onPeriodChange={setPeriod}
        onRetry={() => undefined}
        period={period}
        state={state}
        variant={variant}
      />
    </AppI18nProvider>
  );
}

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Badge, Button, Checkbox, Field, FieldDescription, FieldError, FieldLabel, FormStatus, KeyValueItem, KeyValueList, Notice, Panel, Radio, Select, TextInput } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import { useTheme } from "@creator-signal/ui/client";
import formInventory from "../../../../design-system/form-surfaces.json";
import { referencePath } from "../reference-path";

type FormJourney = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  owner: string;
  source: string;
  storybook: { coverage: "contract" | "excluded" | "rendered"; storyId: string; themePreferences?: Array<"system" | "light" | "dark"> };
  allowedFixtureData: string[];
  providerBoundary: string;
  acceptance: string;
};

const inventory = formInventory as { journeys: FormJourney[] };

function GalleryShell({ children, title }: { children: React.ReactNode; title: string }) {
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Canonical form gallery · synthetic only · no submission</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">{title}</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">This is a review projection of governed primitives, source-owned product journeys and honest provider limits. Storybook has no form runtime dependency, provider access or customer data.</p>{children}</main>;
}

function JourneyContract({ journey }: { journey: FormJourney }) {
  const coverage = journey.storybook.coverage;
  return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><Badge tone={coverage === "rendered" ? "success" : coverage === "excluded" ? "warning" : "info"}>{coverage === "excluded" ? "Tested exclusion" : coverage === "rendered" ? "Rendered reference" : "Integration contract"}</Badge><h2>{journey.label}</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Owner" value={journey.owner} /><KeyValueItem label="Owned source" value={<code>{journey.source}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Fixture boundary" value={journey.allowedFixtureData.join(", ")} /><KeyValueItem label="Acceptance" value={journey.acceptance} /></KeyValueList><p><strong>Provider boundary:</strong> {journey.providerBoundary}</p></Panel>;
}

function SharedWorkflowStatesView() {
  return <GalleryShell title="Shared workflow states"><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid"><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Required and invalid</h2><Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="gallery-email">Email address</FieldLabel><FieldDescription id="gallery-email-help">Synthetic account identity only.</FieldDescription><TextInput id="gallery-email" aria-describedby="gallery-email-help gallery-email-error" aria-invalid="true" defaultValue="maya@example.test" type="email" /><FieldError id="gallery-email-error">Enter a valid email address.</FieldError></Field></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Disabled and read-only</h2><Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="gallery-readonly">Workspace reference</FieldLabel><TextInput id="gallery-readonly" readOnly value="fixture-workspace-001" /></Field><Button disabled>Unavailable while checking</Button></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Async and outcomes</h2><Button busy>Checking synthetic file</Button><FormStatus tone="success">Synthetic check complete. Nothing was uploaded.</FormStatus><FormStatus tone="error">Synthetic request failed. No change was saved.</FormStatus></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Choice controls</h2><Checkbox defaultChecked label="Use this synthetic preference" description="No preference is stored." /><Radio defaultChecked label="Weekly summary" name="gallery-cadence" /><Radio label="Monthly summary" name="gallery-cadence" /></Panel></section></GalleryShell>;
}

function SearchAndFileSelectionView() {
  return <GalleryShell title="Search and file selection"><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid"><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Accessible search</h2><Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="gallery-search">Search synthetic media</FieldLabel><FieldDescription id="gallery-search-help">Filtering is illustrative only; no account or media request is made.</FieldDescription><TextInput id="gallery-search" aria-describedby="gallery-search-help" defaultValue="blue fern" type="search" /></Field><FormStatus>One synthetic result would match this query.</FormStatus></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>File selection</h2><Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="gallery-file">Select a statement file</FieldLabel><FieldDescription id="gallery-file-help">CSV, TSV and text file types are presented by the owning import journey. This fixture never reads or uploads a file.</FieldDescription><TextInput id="gallery-file" aria-describedby="gallery-file-help" accept=".csv,.tsv,.txt,text/csv,text/plain,text/tab-separated-values" type="file" /></Field><Button variant="secondary">Check selected files</Button></Panel></section></GalleryShell>;
}

function StatementImportView() {
  return <GalleryShell title="Statement import integration contract"><section className="cs-story-section"><Notice heading="Owned route, not copied workflow" tone="info"><p>The production component owns drag and drop, preview/import requests, quota calculations and result details. This projection validates the shared field, file-selection, pending and status states without sending a file or duplicating its route logic.</p></Notice></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid">{inventory.journeys.filter(({ id }) => id === "sales-pulse.statement-import").map((journey) => <JourneyContract key={journey.id} journey={journey} />)}</section></GalleryShell>;
}

function LegalConsentView() {

}

  ["prefer_not_to_say", "Prefer not to say"],
  ["does_not_track", "I don't track it"],
  ["spoonflower_dashboard", "Just Spoonflower's own dashboard"],
  ["manual_notes_or_addition", "Manual notes or addition"],

  ["other", "Other"],

function IntakeSelect({ id, label, onChange, options }: { id: string; label: string; onChange?: React.ChangeEventHandler<HTMLSelectElement>; options: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> }) {
  return <Field><FieldLabel htmlFor={id}>{label}</FieldLabel><Select id={id} name={id} onChange={onChange}><option value="">Prefer not to say</option>{options.filter(([value]) => value !== "prefer_not_to_say").map(([value, optionLabel]) => <option key={value} value={value}>{optionLabel}</option>)}</Select></Field>;
}

function IntakeText({ className, id, label, placeholder, required = false, type = "text" }: { className?: string; id: string; label: string; placeholder: string; required?: boolean; type?: "email" | "text" }) {
  return <Field className={className}><FieldLabel htmlFor={id}>{label}</FieldLabel><TextInput id={id} name={id} placeholder={placeholder} required={required} type={type} /></Field>;
}

function IntakeShell({ children, intro, note, submitted, title }: { children: React.ReactNode; intro: string; note?: string; submitted: boolean; title: string }) {
  const { preference, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  const widthClass = title === "Join the waitlist" ? "cs-intake-card--waitlist" : "cs-intake-card--beta";
  const logoSurface = resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "dark-background" : "light-background";
  return <GalleryShell title={`${title} · Mautic design reference`}><section className="cs-story-section cs-intake-reference" data-cs-theme={resolvedTheme} data-cs-theme-preference={preference}><Panel className={`cs-intake-card ${widthClass}`}><header className="cs-intake-heading"><img alt="Creator Signal" className="cs-intake-logo" src={referencePath(`/brand-assets/delivery/creator-signal-logo-stacked/${logoSurface}.svg`)} /><h2>{title}</h2><p>{intro}</p>{note ? <p className="cs-intake-note">{note}</p> : null}</header>{submitted ? <FormStatus tone="success">Synthetic Storybook state only. Nothing was submitted or saved.</FormStatus> : children}</Panel></section></GalleryShell>;
}

function WaitlistView() {
  const [tracking, setTracking] = useState("");
  const [discovery, setDiscovery] = useState("");
  const [otherHelp, setOtherHelp] = useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  return <IntakeShell intro="Be the first to know when Creator Signal is ready." submitted={submitted} title="Join the waitlist"><form className="cs-intake-form" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); setSubmitted(true); }}><IntakeText id="waitlist-first-name" label="First name" placeholder="Your first name" required /><IntakeText id="waitlist-email" label="Email" placeholder={"you\u0040example.com"} required type="email" /><fieldset className="cs-intake-optional"><legend>Tell us a bit more <span>(optional)</span></legend><FieldDescription>Totally optional, but it helps us know who we&apos;re building for.</FieldDescription><IntakeText id="waitlist-shop-name" label="Spoonflower shop name" placeholder="Your shop name" /><IntakeSelect id="waitlist-selling-tenure" label="How long have you been selling on Spoonflower?" options={sellingTenureOptions} /><IntakeSelect id="waitlist-tracking-method" label="How do you currently track your sales?" onChange={(event) => setTracking(event.currentTarget.value)} options={salesTrackingOptions} />{tracking === "other" ? <IntakeText id="waitlist-tracking-method-other" label="Tell us how" placeholder="Tell us how" /> : null}<IntakeSelect id="waitlist-discovery-source" label="How did you hear about us?" onChange={(event) => setDiscovery(event.currentTarget.value)} options={[
    ["instagram", "Instagram"], ["facebook_group", "A Facebook group"], ["friend_or_designer", "A friend or fellow designer"], ["google_search", "Google search"], ["other", "Other"],
  ]} />{discovery === "other" ? <IntakeText id="waitlist-discovery-source-other" label="Tell us where" placeholder="Tell us where" /> : null}<fieldset className="cs-intake-choices"><legend>What would help you most right now?</legend><Checkbox label="Seeing what's actually selling in my shop" name="waitlist-help" value="see_what_sells" /><Checkbox label="Knowing when to upload seasonal designs" name="waitlist-help" value="seasonal_timing" /><Checkbox label="Seeing which category (wallpaper, fabric, home decor) does best" name="waitlist-help" value="category_breakdown" /><Checkbox label="Knowing if people are buying from me again" name="waitlist-help" value="repeat_customers" /><Checkbox label="Just feeling less like I'm guessing" name="waitlist-help" value="stop_guessing" /><Checkbox checked={otherHelp} label="Something else" name="waitlist-help" onChange={(event) => setOtherHelp(event.currentTarget.checked)} value="other" />{otherHelp ? <IntakeText id="waitlist-help-other" label="Tell us what" placeholder="Tell us what" /> : null}</fieldset></fieldset><fieldset className="cs-intake-permission"><legend>Permission to contact you</legend><Checkbox label="Email me one or more updates about Creator Signal launch availability and this waitlist request" name="waitlist-permission" required value="1" /></fieldset><Button type="submit">Join the Waitlist</Button><p className="cs-intake-fineprint">Free to join. This permission is only for Creator Signal launch availability and this waitlist request. <a href="/legal/privacy">Read the Privacy Policy</a>.</p></form></IntakeShell>;
}

function ProviderContract({ id, title }: { id: string; title: string }) {
  const journey = inventory.journeys.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id);
  if (!journey) throw new Error(`missing form journey ${id}`);
  return <GalleryShell title={title}><section className="cs-story-section"><JourneyContract journey={journey} /></section></GalleryShell>;
}

function CatalogueView() {
  return <GalleryShell title="Form journey catalogue"><section className="cs-story-section"><Notice heading="Synthetic fixtures only" tone="info"><p>Each entry identifies the real owner, source, Storybook coverage and acceptance boundary. Provider output is never impersonated.</p></Notice></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid">{inventory.journeys.map((journey) => <JourneyContract key={journey.id} journey={journey} />)}</section></GalleryShell>;
}

const brandTokens = [
  ["Cream", "brand.cream"],
  ["Pink", "brand.pink"],
  ["Orange", "brand.orange"],
  ["Maroon", "brand.maroon"],

  return primitives[name];
}

              <code>{tokenValue(token)}</code>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="cs-radius-row" style={{ marginTop: "var(--cs-spacing-6)" }}>
          {radiusTokens.map((token) => (
            <div className="cs-radius-sample" key={token} style={{ borderRadius: tokenValue(token) }}>
              <code>{token}</code>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="status-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Meaning contract</p>
        <h2 id="status-heading">Status never relies on colour alone</h2>
        <div className="cs-status-grid">
          {(["success", "warning", "error", "info"] as const).map((status) => {
            const hook = meaningHooks[`status.${status}`];
            const symbol = status === "success" ? "✓" : status === "warning" ? "!" : status === "error" ? "×" : "i";
            return (
              <article className={`cs-status-card cs-status-${status}`} key={status}>
                <span className="cs-status-icon" aria-hidden="true">{symbol}</span>
                <div>
                  <strong>{hook.label}</strong>
                  <div className="cs-token-code">Icon hook: {hook.icon}</div>
                  <p>Text and icon meaning remain available when colour cannot be perceived.</p>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="charts-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Data visualisation</p>
        <h2 id="charts-heading">Five governed series with non-colour hooks</h2>
        <div className="cs-chart-bars" role="img" aria-label="Synthetic five-series bar example">
          <div className="cs-chart-bar" />
          <div className="cs-chart-bar" />
          <div className="cs-chart-bar" />
          <div className="cs-chart-bar" />
          <div className="cs-chart-bar" />
        </div>
        <ul className="cs-chart-legend">
          {([1, 2, 3, 4, 5] as const).map((series) => {
            const hook = meaningHooks[`chart.series.${series}`];
            const markerClass = series === 1 ? "circle" : series === 2 ? "square" : series === 3 ? "triangle" : series === 4 ? "diamond" : "cross";
            return (
              <li key={series}>
                <span className={`cs-chart-marker cs-chart-marker-${markerClass}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Series {series}: marker {hook.marker}, line {hook.dash}</span>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <aside className="cs-callout">
        <strong>Foundation boundary</strong>
        <p>
          This Storybook is a review surface only. Production systems consume `@creator-signal/design-system`; they never import Storybook code or fetch tokens from a hosted catalogue.
        </p>
      </aside>
    </main>
  );
}

      }

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Field, FieldLabel, Notice, Select, TextInput } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import {
  InstaticSpecimenStory,
  instaticProjection,
  useFilteredInstaticEntries
} from "../instatic/InstaticSpecimen";

function ImpactGalleryView() {
  const firstEntry = instaticProjection.entries[0];
  if (!firstEntry) throw new Error("Pinned Instatic projection is empty");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [source, setSource] = useState("all");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const filtered = useFilteredInstaticEntries(query, source, category);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(firstEntry.id);
  const selected = filtered.find(({ id }) => id === selectedId) ?? filtered[0];
  const categories = useMemo(() => [...new Set(instaticProjection.entries.map((entry) => entry.category))].sort(), []);

  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell" data-instatic-impact-gallery>
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Consumers · generated catalogue projection · synthetic only</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Instatic Component Library impact gallery</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">Search all {instaticProjection.summary.entryCount} pinned entries. Each result links to a separately generated stable story and previews the real Instatic specimen with current branch design-system assets.</p>
      <Notice heading="Review projection, not runtime authority" tone="info">
        <p>Instatic continues to own CMS fields, patterns, capabilities and HTML. <code>@creator-signal/ui</code> remains the React API authority. Provider-controlled output is labelled and never fabricated.</p>
      </Notice>
      <section className="cs-instatic-gallery-controls" aria-label="Filter the Instatic catalogue">
        <Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="instatic-gallery-query">Search entries</FieldLabel><TextInput id="instatic-gallery-query" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.currentTarget.value)} /></Field>
        <Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="instatic-gallery-source">Source</FieldLabel><Select id="instatic-gallery-source" value={source} onChange={(event) => setSource(event.currentTarget.value)}><option value="all">All sources</option><option value="built-in">Instatic built-in</option><option value="plugin">Creator Signal plugin</option></Select></Field>
        <Field><FieldLabel htmlFor="instatic-gallery-category">Category</FieldLabel><Select id="instatic-gallery-category" value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.currentTarget.value)}><option value="all">All categories</option>{categories.map((value) => <option key={value} value={value}>{value}</option>)}</Select></Field>
      </section>
      <p role="status">Showing {filtered.length} of {instaticProjection.summary.entryCount} entries.</p>
      <section className="cs-instatic-gallery-layout">
        <ul className="cs-instatic-gallery-list" aria-label="Instatic catalogue entries">
          {filtered.map((entry) => <li key={entry.id}><button className="cs-instatic-gallery-entry" type="button" aria-current={selected?.id === entry.id ? "true" : undefined} onClick={() => setSelectedId(entry.id)}><strong>{entry.name}</strong><span>{entry.registryOrigin === "plugin" ? "Creator Signal plugin" : `Built-in · ${entry.category}`}</span><code>{entry.id}</code></button></li>)}
        </ul>
        {selected && selected.scenarios[0] ? <InstaticSpecimenStory embedded entryId={selected.id} scenarioId={selected.scenarios[0].id} /> : <Notice heading="No entries match" tone="warning"><p>Clear one or more filters to continue reviewing the pinned catalogue.</p></Notice>}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

const meta = {
  title: "Consumers/Instatic Component Library",
  tags: ["autodocs"],
  parameters: { docs: { description: { component: "Searchable generated review projection for the complete pinned Instatic executable catalogue." } } }
} satisfies Meta;

export default meta;
type Story = StoryObj<typeof meta>;

export const ImpactGallery: Story = { render: () => <ImpactGalleryView /> };

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { Badge, ResponsiveTable, Table, TableBody, TableCaption, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import migration from "../../migration-status.json";

function tone(status: string): "neutral" | "success" | "warning" | "info" {
  if (status === "migrated") return "success";
  if (status === "in-progress") return "warning";
  if (status === "accepted-no-work") return "info";
  return "neutral";
}

function MigrationStatus() {
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Epic #{migration.epic} projection</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Surface migration status</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">{migration.authority}</p>
      <section className="cs-story-section">
        <ResponsiveTable aria-label="Design-system surface migration status">
          <Table className="cs-migration-table">
            <TableCaption>Current source-controlled Storybook projection for every later consumer in the canonical surface register.</TableCaption>
            <TableHead><TableRow><TableHeader scope="col">Surface</TableHeader><TableHeader scope="col">Status</TableHeader><TableHeader scope="col">Delivery issues</TableHeader></TableRow></TableHead>
            <TableBody>{migration.surfaces.map((surface) => <TableRow key={surface.id}><TableHeader scope="row">{surface.name}</TableHeader><TableCell><Badge tone={tone(surface.status)}>{surface.status}</Badge></TableCell><TableCell>{surface.issues.map((issue) => `#${issue}`).join(", ")}</TableCell></TableRow>)}</TableBody>
          </Table>
        </ResponsiveTable>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

const meta = { title: "Migration status/Canonical surface register", component: MigrationStatus, tags: ["autodocs"] } satisfies Meta<typeof MigrationStatus>;
export default meta;
type Story = StoryObj<typeof meta>;
export const CurrentProjection: Story = {};

function longLabelFixture() {

function ProDashboardPreview({ longLabels = false, state = "ready" }: { longLabels?: boolean; state?: DashboardDataState }) {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<DatePreset>("ytd");
  const [customRange, setCustomRange] = useState({ from: "2026-01-01", to: "2026-04-30" });
  const data = state === "loading" || state === "error" ? null : longLabels ? longLabelFixture() : PRO_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE;
  return (
    <AppI18nProvider locale="en-AU">
      <ProDashboardView
        customRange={customRange}
        data={data}
        {...(state === "error" ? { errorMessage: "Synthetic Storybook error. No marketplace request was made." } : {})}
        onApplyCustomRange={() => undefined}
        onCustomRangeChange={setCustomRange}
        onPeriodChange={setPeriod}
        onRetry={() => undefined}
        period={period}
        state={state}
      />
    </AppI18nProvider>
  );
}

function PatternShell({ children, title, purpose }: { children: React.ReactNode; title: string; purpose: string }) {
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Reusable pattern · synthetic content</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">{title}</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">{purpose}</p>
      <section className="cs-story-section">{children}</section>
    </main>
  );
}

function FormAndAuthentication() {
  return (
    <PatternShell title="Forms and authentication" purpose="Labels, descriptions, validation, status and primary/secondary actions preserve a predictable reading and focus order.">
      <Panel>
        <form className="cs-ui-review-form" onSubmit={(event) => event.preventDefault()}>
          <Field>
            <FieldLabel htmlFor="pattern-email">Email address</FieldLabel>
            <FieldDescription id="pattern-email-help">Use the address connected to your Creator Signal workspace.</FieldDescription>
            <TextInput autoComplete="email" id="pattern-email" aria-describedby="pattern-email-help" defaultValue="maya@example.test" />
          </Field>
          <Field>
            <FieldLabel htmlFor="pattern-code">Verification code</FieldLabel>
            <TextInput autoComplete="one-time-code" id="pattern-code" inputMode="numeric" aria-invalid="true" aria-describedby="pattern-code-error" />
            <FieldError id="pattern-code-error">Enter the six-digit code. No account changes were made.</FieldError>
          </Field>
          <Checkbox label="Trust this browser for 30 days" description="Do not use this option on a shared device." />
          <FormStatus tone="info">A new code can be requested in 24 seconds.</FormStatus>
          <div className="cs-ui-review-form-actions"><Button type="submit">Verify</Button><Button variant="ghost">Use another account</Button></div>
        </form>
      </Panel>
    </PatternShell>
  );
}

    await userEvent.click(trigger);
    await expect(canvas.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Disconnect this synthetic shop?" })).toBeVisible();
    await userEvent.click(canvas.getByRole("button", { name: "Close dialog" }));
    await expect(trigger).toHaveFocus();
  }
};

} as const;

function RoleSpecimens({ fallback = false }: { fallback?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell" data-cs-fonts={fallback ? "fallback" : undefined}>
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Typography · source v{typographySourceVersion}</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Creator Signal typography</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">
        Fredoka carries display and headings, Mulish carries essential interface and data content, and Caveat is reserved for rare non-essential decoration. The font files are self-hosted build assets; no browser font CDN is part of this contract.
      </p>

      {fallback ? (
        <p className="cs-type-fallback-note" role="note">
          <strong>Fallback review:</strong> custom family references are deliberately disabled on this example. Content, hierarchy and numeric meaning must remain readable with the governed system stacks.
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="roles-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Role contract</p>
        <h2 id="roles-heading">Typography roles in context</h2>
        <div className="cs-type-review-grid">
          {roleExamples.map(([roleName, copy]) => {
            const role = typographyRoles[roleName];
            return (
              <article className="cs-type-review-card" key={roleName}>
                <div className="cs-type-meta">
                  <code>{roleName}</code>
                  <span>{role.family}</span>
                  <span>{role.fontWeight}</span>
                  <span>{role.fontSize}</span>
                </div>
                <div className={`cs-type-${roleName.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (match) => `-${match.toLowerCase()}`)}`}>
                  {copy}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="families-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Family and weight boundaries</p>
        <h2 id="families-heading">Only the weights we actually use</h2>
        <div className="cs-type-review-grid">
          {(Object.keys(weightExamples) as Array<keyof typeof weightExamples>).map((familyName) => {
            const family = typographyFamilies[familyName];
            return (
              <article className="cs-type-review-card" key={familyName}>
                <div className="cs-type-meta">
                  <strong>{family.cssFamily}</strong>
                  <span>{family.purpose}</span>
                  <span>{family.weightRange[0]}–{family.weightRange[1]} available</span>
                </div>
                <div className="cs-type-family-specimen" style={{ fontFamily: `var(--cs-font-family-${familyName})` }}>
                  {weightExamples[familyName].map((weight) => (
                    <div className="cs-type-weight-row" key={weight}>
                      <code>{weight}</code>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: weight }}>Creators deserve evidence they can act on.</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="data-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Operational and financial data</p>
        <h2 id="data-heading">Mulish keeps dense information stable</h2>
        <div className="cs-type-review-card" style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
          <table className="cs-type-data-table">
            <caption className="cs-type-body-small" style={{ textAlign: "left", marginBottom: "var(--cs-spacing-3)" }}>
              Synthetic Sales Pulse data for typography review only.
            </caption>
            <thead>
              <tr><th>Design</th><th>Revenue</th><th>Orders</th><th>Change</th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr><td>Botanical block print</td><td>A$2,840.50</td><td>128</td><td>+18.4%</td></tr>
              <tr><td>French pinstripe</td><td>A$1,906.20</td><td>93</td><td>+6.8%</td></tr>
              <tr><td>Textured plaid</td><td>A$986.40</td><td>47</td><td>−2.1%</td></tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="long-copy-heading">
        <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Essential long-form copy</p>
        <h2 id="long-copy-heading">Errors, support and legal text stay calm and readable</h2>
        <div className="cs-type-review-grid">
          <article className="cs-type-review-card">
            <p className="cs-type-label">Error</p>
            <p className="cs-type-body">We couldn’t save this change. Your existing data is unchanged. Try again, or contact support if the problem continues.</p>
          </article>
          <article className="cs-type-review-card">
            <p className="cs-type-label">Support</p>
            <p className="cs-type-body">Need help understanding a recommendation? Open the explanation first, then contact support if the underlying marketplace data still doesn’t look right.</p>
          </article>
          <article className="cs-type-review-card">
            <p className="cs-type-label">Legal</p>
            <p className="cs-type-body-small">Creator Signal provides marketplace intelligence and explainable recommendations to support creator decisions. Recommendations are informational and do not guarantee marketplace performance or future sales.</p>
          </article>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="decorative-heading">

          <span className="cs-type-decorative-sample" aria-hidden="true">a little extra flourish</span>

        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { AppI18nProvider } from "../../../web/i18n/client";
import {
  SALES_INSIGHT_ORDER,
  SalesDashboardView,
  type SalesAccent,
  type SalesDashboardData,
  type SalesDashboardFilters,
  type SalesLayout,
  type SalesSort
} from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/sales/sales-dashboard-view";
import { SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE } from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/sales/sales-dashboard.fixture";
import type { DashboardDataState } from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/_lib/dashboard-composition";
import type { DatePreset } from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard-date-ranges";

const initialFilters: SalesDashboardFilters = {
  from: "2026-01-01",
  to: "2026-04-30",
  category: "",
  format: "",
  material: "",
  search: "",
  repeatCategory: "",
  trendPeriod: "month",
  bestSellersLimit: 10,
  salesLimit: 10,
  repeatCustomersLimit: 10,
  salesSort: "date",
  salesDirection: "desc"
};

function fixtureFor({ imageFallback, limited, longLabels }: { imageFallback: boolean; limited: boolean; longLabels: boolean }): SalesDashboardData {
  const first = SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items[0]!;
  return {
    ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE,
    access: limited ? {
      ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.access,
      mode: "limited",
      searchAllowed: false,
      sortAllowed: false,
      paginationAllowed: false,
      mediaVisible: false,
      exportAllowed: false
    } : SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.access,
    bestSellers: {
      ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.bestSellers,
      items: [{
        ...first,
        designName: longLabels
          ? "A deliberately long synthetic design name that remains readable at narrow widths and two hundred percent browser zoom"
          : first.designName,
        thumbnailUrl: imageFallback ? null : first.thumbnailUrl
      }, ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items.slice(1)]
    },
    repeatCustomers: {
      ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.repeatCustomers,
      items: SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.repeatCustomers.items.map((customer, index) => index === 0 && longLabels
        ? { ...customer, customer: "A deliberately long synthetic customer identity used only to test wrapping and narrow layouts" }
        : customer)
    },
    trends: {
      ...SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.trends,
      rising: SALES_DASHBOARD_FIXTURE.trends.rising.map((item) => ({ ...item, thumbnailUrl: imageFallback ? null : item.thumbnailUrl }))
    }
  };
}

function SalesDashboardPreview({
  imageFallback = false,
  limited = false,
  longLabels = false,
  state = "ready"
}: {
  imageFallback?: boolean;
  limited?: boolean;
  longLabels?: boolean;
  state?: DashboardDataState;
}) {
  const [accent, setAccent] = useState<SalesAccent>("default");
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<SalesLayout>("visual");
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<DatePreset>("ytd");
  const [customRange, setCustomRange] = useState({ from: "2026-01-01", to: "2026-04-30" });
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState(initialFilters);
  const [enabledInsights, setEnabledInsights] = useState([...SALES_INSIGHT_ORDER]);
  const data = state === "loading" || state === "error" ? null : fixtureFor({ imageFallback, limited, longLabels });

  return (
    <AppI18nProvider locale="en-AU">
      <SalesDashboardView
        accent={accent}
        customRange={customRange}
        data={data}
        datePreset={period}
        enabledInsights={enabledInsights}
        {...(state === "error" ? { errorMessage: "Synthetic Storybook error. No marketplace request was made." } : {})}
        filters={filters}
        layout={layout}
        onAccentChange={setAccent}
        onApplyCustomRange={() => setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, ...customRange }))}
        onClearData={() => undefined}
        onCustomRangeChange={setCustomRange}
        onFilterChange={(name, value) => setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }))}
        onImportBackup={() => undefined}
        onLayoutChange={setLayout}
        onPeriodChange={setPeriod}
        onRemoveThumbnail={() => undefined}
        onResetFilters={() => setFilters(initialFilters)}
        onRetry={() => undefined}
        onShowUpgrade={() => undefined}
        onSortSales={(column: SalesSort) => setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, salesSort: column }))}
        onToggleInsight={(insight) => setEnabledInsights((current) => current.includes(insight) ? current.filter((value) => value !== insight) : [...current, insight])}
        onUploadThumbnail={() => undefined}
        state={state}
      />
    </AppI18nProvider>
  );
}

function statusTone(status: string): "success" | "warning" {
  return status === "source-integrated" ? "success" : "warning";
}

function StartHere() {
  const integrated = languageSystem.sections.filter(({ status }) => status === "source-integrated").length;

  return (
    <main className="cs-dls-shell">
      <section className="cs-dls-hero" aria-labelledby="dls-title">
        <div className="cs-dls-hero-copy">

          </div>
        </div>

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { Button, EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingIndicator, Notice, Panel, Skeleton } from "@creator-signal/ui";

type ProductState = "loading" | "empty" | "success" | "warning" | "error" | "entitlement" | "payment-failure" | "verification";

function StatePreview({ state }: { state: ProductState }) {
  const content = {
    loading: <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><LoadingIndicator label="Loading creator signals" /><Skeleton /><Skeleton /></Panel>,
    empty: <EmptyState title="No reports yet" description="Create a report to see useful creator signals." actions={<Button>Create report</Button>} />,

    warning: <Notice heading="Review needed" tone="warning"><p>Two synthetic rows need a category.</p></Notice>,

    entitlement: <Notice heading="Pro feature" tone="warning"><p>Recommendations require Pro. Your current reports remain available.</p><Button variant="secondary">Compare plans</Button></Notice>,
    "payment-failure": <Notice heading="Payment method needs attention" tone="error"><p>Your access continues through 22 August. No charge was made.</p><Button>Update payment method</Button></Notice>,
    verification: <Notice heading="Verify your email" tone="info"><p>Enter the code sent to maya@example.test. This is synthetic fixture data.</p><Button>Enter code</Button></Notice>
  } satisfies Record<ProductState, React.ReactNode>;
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Sales Pulse state matrix · synthetic only</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">{state.replace("-", " ")}</h1><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Current state</h2>{content[state]}</section></main>;
}

function PreviewShell({ children, className = "", label, title }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string; label: string; title: string }) {
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Controlled product preview · synthetic only</p>

import * as React from "react";
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { ThemeControl, ThemeProvider, useTheme } from "@creator-signal/ui/client";
import {
  THEME_STORAGE_KEY,
  createThemeRuntime,
  resolveTheme,
  type ThemePreference
} from "@creator-signal/design-system/theme-runtime";

function createMemoryStorage(initialPreference: ThemePreference) {
  const values = new Map<string, string>();
  if (initialPreference !== "system") values.set(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, initialPreference);
  return {
    getItem(key: string) { return values.get(key) ?? null; },
    setItem(key: string, value: string) { values.set(key, value); },
    removeItem(key: string) { values.delete(key); }
  };
}

        Change the real shared theme preference below. The control persists only a bounded preference, while the runtime resolves system colour scheme and applies the same semantic token API in either theme.
      </p>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="runtime-heading">

function ThemeReviewSurface() {
  const { preference, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  return (
    <div className="cs-story-root" data-cs-theme={resolvedTheme} data-cs-theme-preference={preference}>
      <ThemePanel />

function ThemeRuntimeReview({ initialPreference }: { initialPreference: ThemePreference }) {
  const createRuntime = React.useCallback(() => {
    const media = typeof window.matchMedia === "function" ? window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)") : undefined;
    return createThemeRuntime({
      root: document.documentElement,
      storage: createMemoryStorage(initialPreference),
      ...(media ? { media } : {})
    });
  }, [initialPreference]);

  const initialSnapshot = React.useMemo(() => {
    const systemDark = typeof window.matchMedia === "function"
      ? window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches
      : false;
    return { preference: initialPreference, resolvedTheme: resolveTheme(initialPreference, systemDark) } as const;
  }, [initialPreference]);

  return (
    <ThemeProvider createRuntime={createRuntime} initialSnapshot={initialSnapshot}>
      <ThemeReviewSurface />
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

const meta = {
  title: "Foundations/Theme runtime",
  component: ThemeRuntimeReview,
  tags: ["autodocs"],
  parameters: {
    themeRuntimeOwnsTheme: true,
    docs: {
      description: {
        component: "Executable review of the real #878 framework-neutral runtime and narrow React bindings. System follows the browser/OS preference; Light and Dark prove explicit overrides."
      }
    }
  },
  argTypes: {
    initialPreference: {
      control: "select",
      options: ["system", "light", "dark"]
    }
  }
} satisfies Meta<typeof ThemeRuntimeReview>;

export default meta;

export const Light: Story = { args: { initialPreference: "light" }, globals: { theme: "light" } };

type Background = "light" | "dark" | "transparent";
type ImageState = "present" | "delayed" | "blocked";

const assets = [
  { id: "Creator Signal horizontal", base: "creator-signal-logo-horizontal" },
  { id: "Creator Signal stacked", base: "creator-signal-logo-stacked" },
  { id: "Sales Pulse endorsement", base: "sales-pulse-by-creator-signal-horizontal" },
  { id: "Sales Pulse mark", base: "sales-pulse-mark" }
] as const;

function variantFor(background: Background) {
  if (background === "dark") return "reversed";
  if (background === "transparent") return "one-colour";
  return "light-background";
}

function BrandAssetGallery({ background, imageState }: { background: Background; imageState: ImageState }) {
  const variant = variantFor(background);
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Brand assets · governed output #{manifest.issue}</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Approved identities, rendered from the generated pipeline</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">
        This gallery loads generated delivery assets, not copied Storybook artwork. The manifest currently governs {manifest.outputs.length} technical outputs. Admin and Content use the Creator Signal lockup with a live-text product label.
      </p>
      <section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid" aria-label={`${background} background brand examples`}>
        {assets.map((asset) => (
          <article className="cs-reference-card" key={asset.base}>
            <div className="cs-reference-brand-stage" data-background={background} data-image-state={imageState}>
              <img
                alt={asset.id}
                src={referencePath(`/brand-assets/delivery/${asset.base}/${variant}.svg`)}
              />
              <span className="cs-reference-brand-fallback">{asset.id}</span>
            </div>
            <strong>{asset.id}</strong>
            <code>{asset.base}/{variant}.svg</code>
          </article>
        ))}
        <article className="cs-reference-card">
          <div className="cs-reference-brand-stage" data-background={background} data-image-state={imageState}>

./src/stories/DashboardExplainability.stories.tsx:
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { Badge, EmptyState, ErrorState, KeyValueItem, KeyValueList, LoadingIndicator, Notice, Panel, Skeleton, Stat, StatGroup, StatusIndicator } from "@creator-signal/ui";
import { adminRegisteredCustomersMetric, proCategoryLeadRule, proNetEarningsMetric } from "@sales-pulse/analytics";
import { dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue } from "@sales-pulse/dashboard-contracts";
import catalogue from "../../../../dashboard-explainability/catalogue.json";

type DataPoint = { id: string; label: string; sourceRefs: string[]; metricRefs: string[]; ruleRefs: string[] };
type Widget = { id: string; label: string; classification: string; presentationOnly?: boolean; component: string; selector: string; api?: { endpoint: string; responseFields: string[]; responseTypes: string[] }; filters: string[]; supportedStates: string[]; dataPoints: DataPoint[] };
type Section = { id: string; label: string; selector: string; widgets: Widget[] };
type Dashboard = { id: string; label: string; classification: string; lineageConfidence: string; route: string; component: string; selector: string; sections: Section[] };
type Definition = { name: string; explanation: string; semantics: { window: string; timezone: string; freshness: string; missingData: string }; authority?: string };
type ReferenceState = "normal" | "loading" | "empty" | "warning" | "error" | "entitlement" | "stale";

const generatedCatalogue = catalogue as unknown as { dashboards: Dashboard[] };
const definitions = new Map<string, Definition>([
  ["revenue.net@1", proNetEarningsMetric],
  ["signal.category-lead@1", proCategoryLeadRule],
  ["admin.registered_users@1", adminRegisteredCustomersMetric]
]);

const sourceById = new Map(dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue.sources.map((source) => [source.id, source]));
const acquisitionBySource = new Map(dashboardExplainabilityCatalogue.acquisitionPaths.map((path) => [path.sourceRef, path]));

function pilotWidgets() {
  return generatedCatalogue.dashboards.flatMap((dashboard) => dashboard.sections.flatMap((section) => section.widgets.map((widget) => ({ dashboard, section, widget })))).filter(({ widget }) => Boolean(widget.api));
}

function stateContent(state: ReferenceState) {
  if (state === "loading") return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><LoadingIndicator label="Loading synthetic dashboard data" /><Skeleton /><Skeleton /></Panel>;
  if (state === "empty") return <EmptyState title="No synthetic records in this period" description="The reference shows the empty state without inventing a value." />;
  if (state === "warning") return <Notice heading="Partial synthetic evidence" tone="warning"><p>One source is delayed. Labels and timestamps make the limitation explicit; colour is not the only signal.</p></Notice>;
  if (state === "error") return <ErrorState title="Synthetic source unavailable" description="No data was replaced or estimated. Try again when the source is available." />;
  if (state === "entitlement") return <Notice heading="Synthetic entitlement boundary" tone="warning"><p>This widget is unavailable until the required plan or operator permission is present.</p></Notice>;
  if (state === "stale") return <Notice heading="Synthetic stale result" tone="info"><p>This view uses an older generated timestamp and must be labelled before use.</p></Notice>;
  return <Notice heading="Synthetic populated state" tone="success"><p>Example values are invented review fixtures, never customer data.</p></Notice>;
}

function Lineage({ dashboard, widget }: { dashboard: Dashboard; widget: Widget }) {
  const point = widget.dataPoints[0]!;
  const sourceRef = point.sourceRefs[0] ?? "";
  const source = sourceById.get(sourceRef);
  const acquisition = acquisitionBySource.get(sourceRef);
  const calculation = [...point.metricRefs, ...point.ruleRefs].join(" + ") || "configuration-only presentation";
  return <div className="cs-lineage" role="img" aria-label={`${dashboard.label}, ${widget.label}: dashboard to widget to API to calculation to storage to acquisition lineage`}><strong>Generated lineage (text alternative included)</strong><ol><li>{dashboard.label}</li><li>{widget.label}</li><li>{widget.api?.endpoint ?? "No API; static configuration"}</li><li>{calculation}</li><li>{source?.location ?? point.sourceRefs.join(", ")}</li><li>{acquisition?.label ?? "Repository configuration"}</li></ol></div>;
}

function DashboardSummary({ dashboard }: { dashboard: Dashboard }) {
  const widgets = dashboard.sections.flatMap((section) => section.widgets);
  return <Panel className="cs-reference-card"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Generated dashboard summary</p><h2>{dashboard.label}</h2><p>{dashboard.classification === "repository_configured_upstream" ? "Presentation-only configured operator links. No unavailable upstream internals are inferred." : "Application-owned surface described by accepted manifest fields and identifiers."}</p><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Stable ID" value={<code>{dashboard.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Route" value={<code>{dashboard.route}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Source component" value={<code>{dashboard.component}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Manifested widgets" value={String(widgets.length)} /></KeyValueList></Panel>;
}

function WidgetReference({ dashboard, section, widget, state = "normal" }: { dashboard: Dashboard; section: Section; widget: Widget; state?: ReferenceState }) {
  const point = widget.dataPoints[0]!;
  const sources = point.sourceRefs.map((id) => sourceById.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const acquisitions = point.sourceRefs.map((id) => acquisitionBySource.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const definitionEntries = [...point.metricRefs, ...point.ruleRefs].map((reference) => [reference, definitions.get(reference)] as const);
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Dashboard explainability · synthetic static reference</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">{widget.label}</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">{dashboard.label} / {section.label}. This page is generated from accepted dashboard manifests, metric/rule contracts and source/acquisition contracts. It never connects to a customer session or provider.</p><section className="cs-story-section"><Lineage dashboard={dashboard} widget={widget} /></section><section className="cs-story-section cs-reference-grid"><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Purpose and visible part</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Widget ID" value={<code>{widget.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Data point ID" value={<code>{point.id}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="Rendered selector" value={<code>{widget.selector}</code>} /><KeyValueItem label="API fields" value={<ul className="cs-reference-code-list">{widget.api?.responseFields.map((field: string) => <li key={field}><code>{field}</code></li>)}</ul>} /></KeyValueList></Panel><Panel className="cs-reference-card"><h2>Window, freshness and caveats</h2><KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Filters" value={widget.filters.length ? widget.filters.join(", ") : "No user-selected filters"} /><KeyValueItem label="Supported states" value={widget.supportedStates.join(", ")} /><KeyValueItem label="Confidence" value={dashboard.lineageConfidence} /><KeyValueItem label="Caveat" value={widget.presentationOnly ? "Presentation-only configuration; no product metric is claimed." : "Synthetic examples preserve the contract and do not show customer records."} /></KeyValueList></Panel></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Calculation and version</h2><div className="cs-reference-grid">{definitionEntries.map(([reference, definition]) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={reference}><Badge tone={definition ? "success" : "neutral"}>{reference}</Badge><h3>{definition?.name ?? "Supporting contract"}</h3><p>{definition?.explanation ?? "This supporting reference remains explicitly named but is outside the versioned pilot definition set."}</p>{definition && <KeyValueList><KeyValueItem label="Authority" value={definition.authority ?? "rule"} /><KeyValueItem label="Window" value={definition.semantics.window} /><KeyValueItem label="Timezone" value={definition.semantics.timezone} /><KeyValueItem label="Freshness" value={definition.semantics.freshness} /><KeyValueItem label="Missing data" value={definition.semantics.missingData} /></KeyValueList>}</Panel>)}</div></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Source and acquisition evidence</h2><div className="cs-reference-grid">{sources.map((source) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={source!.id}><h3>{source!.id}</h3><p><code>{source!.location}</code></p><p>{source!.confidence} · {source!.sensitivity}</p></Panel>)}{acquisitions.map((path) => <Panel className="cs-reference-card" key={path!.id}><h3>{path!.label}</h3><ol>{path!.steps.map((step) => <li key={step}>{step}</li>)}</ol><p><strong>Evidence:</strong> <code>{path!.evidenceLocation}</code></p></Panel>)}</div></section><section className="cs-story-section"><h2>Synthetic isolated-widget state</h2>{stateContent(state)}</section></main>;
}

function DashboardOverview() {
  return <main className="cs-story-shell"><p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Dashboard explainability · synthetic static reference</p><h1 className="cs-story-title">Dashboard parts and lineage</h1><p className="cs-story-lede">This reference is a generated review aid. function DashboardPatternPreview({ state = "ready" }: { state?: "loading" | "updating" | "empty" | "error" | "ready" }) {

  return (

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import { AppI18nProvider } from "../../../web/i18n/client";
import {
  ShopOverviewDashboardView,
  type OverviewDashboardData,
  type OverviewDashboardVariant
} from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/overview/shop-overview-client";
import { SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE } from "../../../web/app/sales-pulse/spoonflower/dashboard/overview/shop-overview.fixture";

type PreviewState = "loading" | "updating" | "empty" | "error" | "ready";

function fixtureFor({ imageFallback, longLabel }: { imageFallback: boolean; longLabel: boolean }): OverviewDashboardData {
  const first = SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items[0]!;
  return {
    ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE,
    bestSellers: {
      ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers,
      items: [{
        ...first,
        designName: longLabel
          ? "A deliberately long synthetic Spoonflower design name for narrow layouts and two hundred percent zoom"
          : first.designName,
        thumbnailUrl: imageFallback ? null : first.thumbnailUrl
      }, ...SHOP_OVERVIEW_FIXTURE.bestSellers.items.slice(1)]
    }
  };
}

function DashboardPreview({ imageFallback = false, longLabel = false, state = "ready", variant = "overview" }: {
  imageFallback?: boolean;
  longLabel?: boolean;
  state?: PreviewState;
  variant?: OverviewDashboardVariant;
}) {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<"30" | "month" | "ytd" | "lastyear" | "all">("ytd");
  const [chartMode, setChartMode] = useState<"revenue" | "orders" | "both">("both");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<"Wallpaper" | "Fabric" | "Home decor" | "">("");
  const [format, setFormat] = useState("");
  const [highlightIndex, setHighlightIndex] = useState(0);
  const [accent, setAccent] = useState<"pink" | "orange" | "periwinkle" | "green" | "sand">("pink");
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState<"thumbnails" | "rows" | "columns">("thumbnails");
  const hasData = state !== "loading" && state !== "error";
  const data = hasData ? fixtureFor({ imageFallback, longLabel }) : null;

  return (
    <AppI18nProvider locale="en-AU">
      <ShopOverviewDashboardView
        accent={accent}
        category={category}
        chartMode={chartMode}
        data={data}
        {...(state === "error" ? { errorMessage: "Synthetic Storybook error. No marketplace request was made." } : {})}
        format={format}
        highlightIndex={highlightIndex}
        layout={layout}
        onAccentChange={setAccent}
        onCategoryChange={(value) => { setCategory(value); setFormat(""); }}
        onChartModeChange={setChartMode}
        onFormatChange={(value, nextFormat) => { setCategory(value); setFormat(nextFormat); }}
        onHighlightChange={setHighlightIndex}
        onLayoutChange={setLayout}
        onPeriodChange={setPeriod}
        onRetry={() => undefined}
        period={period}
        state={state}
        variant={variant}
      />
    </AppI18nProvider>
  );
}

import type { Meta, StoryObj } from "@storybook/react-vite";
import {
  designSystemSourceVersion,
  meaningHooks,
  primitives
} from "@creator-signal/design-system";

const brandTokens = [
  ["Cream", "brand.cream"],
  ["Pink", "brand.pink"],
  ["Orange", "brand.orange"],
  ["Maroon", "brand.maroon"],
  ["Periwinkle", "brand.periwinkle"],
  ["Sales Pulse green", "brand.salesPulseGreen"]
] as const;

const spacingTokens = ["spacing.1", "spacing.2", "spacing.3", "spacing.4", "spacing.6", "spacing.8", "spacing.12", "spacing.16"] as const;
const radiusTokens = ["radius.sm", "radius.md", "radius.lg", "radius.pill"] as const;

function tokenValue(name: keyof typeof primitives) {
  return primitives[name];
}

function FoundationGallery() {
  return (
    <main className="cs-story-shell">
      <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Foundation · source v{designSystemSourceVersion}</p>
      <h1 className="cs-story-title">Creator Signal design system</h1>
      <p className="cs-story-lede">
        This executable foundation begins with the governed #876 semantic tokens and now uses the #877 typography contract. Use the Storybook theme toolbar to compare the same semantic API in light and dark mode; shared production UI components deliberately arrive under #879 rather than being faked here.
      </p>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="palette-heading">
        <div className="cs-story-section-header">
          <div>
            <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Brand primitives</p>
            <h2 id="palette-heading">Approved palette</h2>
          </div>
          <p className="cs-story-muted">Guideline values are authoritative; legacy SVG hex values are not.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="cs-token-grid">
          {brandTokens.map(([label, token]) => (
            <article className="cs-swatch" key={token}>
              <div className="cs-swatch-color" style={{ background: tokenValue(token) }} />
              <div className="cs-swatch-copy">
                <strong>{label}</strong>
                <code>{token}</code>
                <code>{tokenValue(token)}</code>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="cs-story-section" aria-labelledby="semantic-heading">
        <div className="cs-story-section-header">
          <div>
            <p className="cs-story-eyebrow">Semantic layer</p>
            <h2 id="semantic-heading">Purpose, not colour names</h2>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="cs-semantic-grid">
          <article className="cs-semantic-card">
            <div className="cs-semantic-sample" style={{ background: "var(--cs-surface-canvas)" }}>
              Canvas surface
            </div>
            <strong>surface.canvas</strong>
            <div className="cs-token-code">--cs-surface-canvas</div>
          </article>

          </article>

          </article>

      </section>

              <code>{tokenValue(token)}</code>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="cs-radius-row" style={{ marginTop: "var(--cs-spacing-6)" }}>
          {radiusTokens.map((token) => (
            <div className="cs-radius-sample" key={token} style={{ borderRadius: tokenValue(token) }}>
              <code>{token}</code>
